<script type="text/javascript">

(function($, A){
    var initGA = function() {
        if (!window.EPCOREGAInstance && (false == false || false == true)) {
                        var propertiesStr = 'KRDO:UA-19609167-1,Client:UA-19608492-1,KRDO2:UA-17425661-1';
            var globalPropertiesStr = 'LAKANA:UA-18558019-8';
            var properties = [];
            var arr = [];

            if (globalPropertiesStr) {
                arr = globalPropertiesStr.split(':');
                if (arr) {
                                            properties.push({'name': arr[0], 'propertyId': arr[1], 'demographics': true});
                                    }
            }

            if (propertiesStr) {
                var propertiesStrArr = propertiesStr.split(',');
                if (propertiesStrArr) {
                    for (var i in propertiesStrArr) {
                        arr = propertiesStrArr[i].split(':');
                                                    properties.push({'name': arr[0], 'propertyId': arr[1], 'demographics': true});
                                            }
                }
            }

                            for (var i in properties) {
                    properties[i].domainName = 'krdo.com';
                }
            
            
            var fields =[];

            var GA = new EP.Components.Analytics.GA(null, properties, fields);
            window.EPCOREGAInstance = GA;
        }
    };
    /**
     * Global event tracking function
     * @param category {string} - eventCategory
     * @param action {string} - eventAction
     * @param eventLabel {string} - eventLabel - optional
     * @param val {number} - eventValue - optional
     * @param fields {Object} - a map of field name/value pairs, such as, dimensions
     */
    window.EPCOREGAEvent = function (category, action, eventLabel, val, fields) {
        if (window.EPCOREGAInstance) {
            window.EPCOREGAInstance.event(category, action, eventLabel, val, fields);
        } else {
            AUI().use('google-analytics', function(A) {
                initGA();
                if (window.EPCOREGAInstance) {
                    window.EPCOREGAInstance.event(category, action, eventLabel, val, fields);
                }
            });
        }
    };
    /**
     * Global virtual page view function
     * @param page {string} - the virtual page to override current page
     * @param fields {Object} - a map of field name/value pairs, such as, page level dimensions
     */
    window.EPCOREVirtualPageview = function (page, fields) {
        if (window.EPCOREGAInstance) {
            window.EPCOREGAInstance.virtualPageview(page, fields);
        } else {
            AUI().use('google-analytics', function(A) {
                initGA();
                if (window.EPCOREGAInstance) {
                    window.EPCOREGAInstance.virtualPageview(page, fields);
                }
            });
        }
    };

    AUI().use('google-analytics', function(A) {
        var siteName = 'KRDO';
        var contentType = '';
        var author = 'none';
        var cmsContentId = 'none';
        var source = 'none';
        var category = 'none';
        var cmsInstance = 'none';
        var externalId = 'none';
        var platform = 'responsive';
                    contentType = 'index';
            author = 'none';
            cmsContentId = '';
            source = 'none';
            category = 'Home';
            cmsInstance = 'core';
            externalId = '';
            if(contentType == null || contentType == '') {
                contentType = 'article';
            }
            if(author == null || author == '') {
                author = 'none';
            }
            if(cmsContentId == null || cmsContentId == '') {
                cmsContentId = 'none';
            }
            if(source == null || source == ''){
                source = 'none';
            }
            if(category == null || category == ''){
                source = 'none';
            }
            if(cmsInstance == null || cmsInstance == ''){
                cmsInstance = 'LAKANA';
            }
            if(externalId == null || externalId == ''){
                externalId = 'none';
            }
        
        var customFields =[
            {'name': 'dimension1', 'value': cmsInstance},
            {'name': 'dimension2', 'value': siteName},
            {'name': 'dimension3', 'value': category},
            {'name': 'dimension4', 'value': contentType},
            {'name': 'dimension5', 'value': cmsContentId},
            {'name': 'dimension6', 'value': source},
            {'name': 'dimension7', 'value': author},
            {'name': 'dimension8', 'value': externalId},
            {'name': 'dimension9', 'value': platform}
        ];

        initGA();
        if (window.EPCOREGAInstance) {
            window.EPCOREGAInstance.pageview(customFields);
        }
    });
})(jQuery, AUI());

                        (function($, A){
                            window.setTimeout(function() {
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            var searchMobileForm = $('#site-search-mobile__form'),
                                        searchMobileField = $('#site-search-mobile__field'),
                                        searchMobileBtn = $('#site-search-mobile__button');

                                    var submitSearch = function (e) {
                                        e.preventDefault();
                                        var t = searchMobileField.val().trim();
                                        if (t !== '') {
                                            window.location = "/search?q=" + t;
                                        }
                                    };

                                    searchMobileBtn.on('click', submitSearch);
                                    searchMobileForm.on('submit', submitSearch);
                                    searchMobileField.on('keydown', function (e) {
                                        if (e.keyCode === 13) {
                                            submitSearch(e);
                                        }
                                    });
                                                            }, 0);
                        })( jQuery, AUI() );

<div class="portlet-body">
<div class="portlet-borderless-container" style="">
<div class="portlet-body">
<div class="journal-content-article" id="article_10141821_32621248_M-BREAKING-NEWS_1.0">
</div>

<div class="portlet-boundary portlet-boundary_56_ portlet-static portlet-static-end portlet-borderless portlet-journal-content" id="p_p_id_56_INSTANCE_mnb0qep2bn3Q_">
<span id="p_56_INSTANCE_mnb0qep2bn3Q"></span>
<div class="portlet-body">
<div class="portlet-borderless-container" style="">
<div class="portlet-body">
<div class="journal-content-article" id="article_10141821_32621248_50749086_1.2">
<section class="module mod-alert wasp" style="background-color: rgb(218, 13, 13);"><header class="mod-header"><h2>WEATHER ALERT</h2></header><div class="mod-content"><div class="item alert-box"><a href="https://www.krdo.com/weather/alerts"><h2 class="message">There are 24 areas under alert. Weather Alerts Details</h2></a></div></div></section><script class="wxAlertScript" id="wxAlertScript-mod-amino-alert50749086" type="text/javascript">
        (function ($, A) {
            var scriptEl = $('#wxAlertScript-mod-amino-alert50749086'),
                config = {
                    displayAsCarousel: false,
                    alertBarText: 'number_of_areas',
                    textUsed: 'story_title',
                    customText: '',
                    useDefaultImage: false,
                    defaultImage: '',
                    color: '#da0d0d',
                    displayBySeverity: false
                },
                model;
            /* BEGIN Carousel, if configured */
            var renderWxAlertCarouselItem = function (item) {
                var carouselItem = '<div class="item alert-box"><h2 class="message">';
                carouselItem += '<a href="https://www.krdo.com/weather/alerts" title="' + item.alertDesc + '">';
                carouselItem += item.alertDesc + ': ';

                item.zones.forEach(function(zone, idx) {
                    carouselItem += zone;
                    if (idx < item.zones.length - 1) {
                        carouselItem += ', ';
                    }
                });

                carouselItem += '</a></h2></div>';

                return carouselItem;
            };
            var initWxAlertCarousel = function (el) {
                var afterAction = function (el) {
                    var actualValue = this.owl.currentItem + 1;
                    el.siblings('.carousel-counter').find('.page-current').text(actualValue);
                };
                var carouselConfig = {
                    navigation : true,
                    singleItem : true,
                    autoHeight : true,
                    afterAction : afterAction
                };
                A.use('owl', function(){
                    el.owlCarousel(carouselConfig);
                });
            };
            var renderWxAlertCarousel = function () {
                var counterEls,
                    carouselItems = '',
                    modHeaderEl = '<header class="mod-header"><h2>WEATHER ALERT</h2></header>',
                    carouselWrapEl = $('<div class="owl-carousel owl-theme"></div>'),
                    modContentEl = $('<div class="mod-content"></div>'),
                    modWrapEl = $('<section class="module mod-alert mod-carousel controls-middle wasp" data-delay-milliseconds="5000"></section>')
                                    .css('background-color', config.color);

                if (model.totalCount > 1) {
                    counterEls = '<div class="carousel-counter"><div class="page-counter">';
                    counterEls += '<span class="page-current">1</span>&nbsp;/';
                    counterEls += '<span class="page-total">' + model.totalCount + '</span>';
                    counterEls += '</div></div>';
                }

                if (config.displayBySeverity) {
                    var severityOrder = ['Extreme', 'Severe', 'Moderate', 'Minor', 'Unknown'];
                    $.each(model.wxAlertMasterBean.weatherAlerts, function(idx, item){
                        var sIdx = severityOrder.indexOf(item.severity);
                        item.severityRank = sIdx < 0 ? 100 : sIdx;
                    });
                    model.wxAlertMasterBean.weatherAlerts.sort(compareBySeverityAndAlphabet);
                }

                $.each(model.wxAlertMasterBean.weatherAlerts, function (idx, item) {
                    carouselItems += renderWxAlertCarouselItem(item);
                });

                carouselWrapEl.append(carouselItems);
                modContentEl.append(carouselWrapEl).append(counterEls);
                modWrapEl.append(modHeaderEl).append(modContentEl);
                scriptEl.before(modWrapEl);
                initWxAlertCarousel(carouselWrapEl);
            };

            var compareBySeverityAndAlphabet = function (a, b) {
                if (a.severityRank != b.severityRank) {
                    return a.severityRank - b.severityRank;
                } else {
                    return a.alertDesc > b.alertDesc ? 1 : -1;
                }
            }
            /* END Carousel, if configured */

            /* BEGIN non-carousel alert, if configured */
            var getWxAlertMsg = function () {
                var message = '';
                var handleCustomText = function (primaryTotal, secondaryTotal) {
                    var replaced = config.customText.replace('%', primaryTotal); /* Use primaryTotal to replace first token... not using a regex, to ensure that only the first token is targeted. */
                    replaced = replaced.replace('%', secondaryTotal); /* If second token exists, replace with secondaryTotal */
                    return replaced;
                };
                var assembleMsg = function (key, primaryTotal, secondaryTotal) {
                    var numAlertsKey = primaryTotal === 1 ? 'single' : 'multiple',
                        defaultMsg = {
                            numberOfAlerts: {
                                single: 'There is 1 weather alert.',
                                multiple: 'There are ' + primaryTotal + ' weather alerts.'
                            },
                            numberOfAreasWithAlerts: {
                                single: 'There is 1 area with 1 active weather alert.',
                                singleAreaWithMultiple: 'There is 1 area with ' + secondaryTotal + ' active weather alerts.',
                                multiple: 'There are ' + primaryTotal + ' areas with ' + secondaryTotal + ' active weather alerts.'
                            },
                            numberOfAreas: {
                                single: 'There is 1 area under alert.',
                                multiple: 'There are ' + primaryTotal + ' areas under alert.'
                            }
                        };
                    if (key === 'numberOfAreasWithAlerts' && primaryTotal === 1 && secondaryTotal > 1) {
                        numAlertsKey = 'singleAreaWithMultiple';
                    }
                    if (config.textUsed === 'custom_text') {
                        message = handleCustomText(primaryTotal, secondaryTotal);
                    } else {
                        message = defaultMsg[key][numAlertsKey];
                    }
                };
                switch (config.alertBarText) {
                    case 'number_of_alerts':
                        assembleMsg('numberOfAlerts', model.totalCount, model.totalCount);
                        break;
                    case 'number_of_areas_with_alerts':
                        assembleMsg('numberOfAreasWithAlerts', model.totalTargetAreas, model.totalCount);
                        break;
                    default: /* "number_of_areas" */
                        assembleMsg('numberOfAreas', model.totalTargetAreas, model.totalCount);
                        break;
                }
                return message + ' Weather Alerts Details';
            };
            var getWxAlertImg = function () {
                var imgEls = '';
                if (config.useDefaultImage) {
                    var imgEl = '<img src="https://www.krdo.com/npg-main-theme/images/placeholder/placeholder-1x1.jpg" alt="" />',
                        cropPhotoEl = $('<div class="crop-photo image"></div>')
                            .append(imgEl)
                            .css('background-image', 'url("")');
                    imgEls = $('<div class="image-wrapper"></div>')
                        .append(cropPhotoEl);
                }
                return imgEls;
            };
            var renderWxAlertMsg = function () {
                var modMessageEl = '<h2 class="message">' + getWxAlertMsg() + '</h2>',
                    modLinkEl = $('<a href="https://www.krdo.com/weather/alerts"></a>')
                        .append(getWxAlertImg())
                        .append(modMessageEl),
                    modAlertItemEl = $('<div class="item alert-box"></div>')
                        .append(modLinkEl),
                    modContentEl = $('<div class="mod-content"></div>')
                        .append(modAlertItemEl),
                    modHeaderEl = '<header class="mod-header"><h2>WEATHER ALERT</h2></header>',
                    modWrapEl = $('<section class="module mod-alert wasp"></section>')
                        .append(modHeaderEl)
                        .append(modContentEl)
                        .css('background-color', config.color);

                scriptEl.before(modWrapEl);
            };
            /* END non-carousel alert, if configured */
            var renderWxServiceAlertsViewFromModel = function () {
                model = EP.WeatherService.model;
                if (model.hasAlerts) {
                    if (config.displayAsCarousel) {
                        renderWxAlertCarousel();
                    } else {
                        renderWxAlertMsg();
                    }
                }
            };
            /**
             * Instantiate via EP.WeatherService.loadQueueCallback to ensure ajax loaded data is ready for display
             */
            if (EP && EP.WeatherService) {
                EP.WeatherService.loadQueueCallback(renderWxServiceAlertsViewFromModel);
            }
        })(jQuery, AUI());
    </script>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<script>
        (function($) {
            var listSelector = $("#playlist_34581.mod-media .mod-content .media-sidebar"),
                hoverArrows = $("#playlist_34581.mod-media .scroll-arrows"),
                previous = hoverArrows.find(".prev"),
                next = hoverArrows.find(".next"),
                scrollableArea = listSelector.find("ul");
            var snapScroll = function (direction) {
                var scrollWidth = scrollableArea.width();
                if (direction === 'left') {
                    scrollableArea.animate({scrollLeft: '-=' + scrollWidth});
                } else if (direction === 'right'){
                    scrollableArea.animate({scrollLeft: '+=' + scrollWidth});
                }
            };
            var recalcScrollPos = EP.Utils.Debounce(function () {
                if (hoverArrows.is(":visible")) {
                    var scrollWidth = scrollableArea.width(),
                        scrollPos = scrollableArea.scrollLeft(),
                        scrollPanePos = Math.round(scrollPos / scrollWidth);
                    scrollableArea.animate({scrollLeft: (scrollWidth * scrollPanePos)});
                }
            }, 500);
            previous.on("click", function () {
                snapScroll('left');
            });
            next.on("click", function () {
                snapScroll('right');
            });
            $(window).on('resize', recalcScrollPos);

        })(jQuery);
    </script>

        var autoflowId = $(this).children("option:selected").val();
        var ajaxUrl = $(this).attr("ajaxUrl");
        var tmpLimit = $(this).children("option:selected").attr("data-limit-number");
        var url = ajaxUrl + autoflowId;

        content.empty();
        content.append("<div class='loader'><img src='https://www.krdo.com/npg-main-theme/images/loader.gif' alt='Loading'></div>");

            type : "GET",

        }).done(function(html) {

(function($, A){
    $("#filter-mod-filtered-story-list-79031794").change(function(){
        var autoflowId = $(this).children("option:selected").val();
        var ajaxUrl = $(this).attr("ajaxUrl");
		var tmpLimit = $(this).children("option:selected").attr("data-limit-number");
        var url = ajaxUrl + autoflowId;
		url = url.replace(/tmpLimitNum/, tmpLimit);
                var content = $("#content-mod-filtered-story-list-79031794");
        content.empty();
        content.append("<div class='loader'><img src='https://www.krdo.com/npg-main-theme/images/loader.gif' alt='Loading'></div>");
        $.ajax({
            type : "GET",
            url : url
        }).done(function(html) {
            var contentHTML = $(html);
            content.empty();
            content.append(contentHTML);
        }).fail(function() {
            content.append("<p class='text-center'>Oops. Something went wrong. Please wait a minute and refresh your browser.</p>")
        });
    });
})(jQuery, AUI());

<footer autoflowid="1136062865" class="mod-footer text-center" id="load-more-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527" pagenum="2">
<span>
<a ajaxurl="/refresh-article/32627301/58906527/AMINO_INFINITE_SCROLLING_MEDIA_HEADLINE_LIST_PAGINATION?pageSize=15&amp;ad=&amp;tmpLimit=15&amp;" class="button" href="">Load More</a>
</span>
</footer>
</section>
</div>
<script>
(function($, A){
    var loadMore = $('#load-more-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527'),
        alink = loadMore.find('a.button'),
        contentMod = $('#content-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527');

    alink.click(function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var ajaxUrl = alink.attr("ajaxUrl"),
            pageNum = parseInt(loadMore.attr("pageNum")),
            autoflowId = loadMore.attr("autoflowId");

        if (true) {
            autoflowId = $("#filter-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527").val();
        }
                if(alink.hasClass("disabled")) {
            return;
        }
        contentMod.append("<div class='loader'><img src='https://www.krdo.com/npg-main-theme/images/loader.gif' alt='Loading'></div>");
        alink.addClass("disabled");
        if (isNaN(pageNum)) {
            return;
        }
        var url = ajaxUrl + "autoflowId=" + autoflowId + "&pageNum=" + pageNum;
        if (true) {
            var tmpLimit = $("#filter-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527").find("option:selected").attr("data-limit-number");
            url = url.replace(url.substring(url.indexOf("tmpLimit"), url.indexOf("&", url.indexOf("tmpLimit"))), "tmpLimit=" + tmpLimit);
        }
        $.ajax({
            type : "GET",
            url : url
        }).done(function(html) {
            pageNum++;
            loadMore.attr("pageNum", pageNum);
            var contentHTML = $(html).filter("ul"),
                stories = $('#content-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527').children('ul').first();

            /* Pass along classes so the formats match */
            contentHTML.attr( 'class', stories.attr('class') );

            contentMod.find('.loader').remove();
            contentMod.append(contentHTML);
            alink.removeClass("disabled");
            $(window).trigger('ep-imgloadgroup-refresh');
        }).fail(function() {
            contentMod.append("<p class='text-center'>Oops. Something went wrong. Please wait a minute and refresh your browser.</p>");
        });
    });
    $("#filter-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527").change(function(){
        loadMore.attr("pageNum", 2);
        var autoflowId = $(this).children("option:selected").val();
        loadMore.attr("autoflowId", autoflowId);
        var ajaxUrl = $(this).attr("ajaxUrl");
        var tmpLimit = $(this).children("option:selected").attr("data-limit-number");
        var url = ajaxUrl + autoflowId;
        url = url.replace(/tmpLimitNum/, tmpLimit);
                loadMore.hide();
        loadMore.children('a').removeClass("disabled");
        var content = $("#content-mod-infinite-scrolling-media-headline-list-58906527");
        contentMod.empty();
        contentMod.append("<div class='loader'><img src='https://www.krdo.com/npg-main-theme/images/loader.gif' alt='Loading'></div>");
        $.ajax({
            type : "GET",
            url : url
        }).done(function(html) {
            var contentHTML = $(html).filter("ul"),
                currentClasses = contentMod.children('ul').first().attr('class');
            contentMod.empty();

            /* Pass along classes so the formats match */
            contentHTML.attr( 'class', currentClasses );
            
            contentMod.append(contentHTML);
            $(window).trigger('ep-imgloadgroup-refresh');
            loadMore.show();
        }).fail(function() {
            contentMod.append("<p class='text-center'>Oops. Something went wrong. Please wait a minute and refresh your browser.</p>");
        });
    });
})(jQuery, AUI());
</script>
